- **cso-detection-rule**: CSO track project #6. Auto-load when the learner is in `cybersecurity-ops/cso-detection-rule` or asks to learn how to write Sentinel analytics rules, scheduled queries, entity mapping, impossible travel detection, or tuning a SIEM detection. ([jay-steenbergen/MSSAMentorAgent](https://github.com/jay-steenbergen/MSSAMentorAgent/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/tracks/cybersecurity-ops/cso-detection-rule) · ★2)
